### **Expanded Version of the Current Scenario**

#### **Scenario: Rollback with Nested Transactions and Selective State Restoration**

```json
{
"id": "test-nested-transaction-rollback",
"type": "Scenario",
"description": "Rollback with nested transactions and selective state restoration",
"stages": [
{
"stage": "NESTED_TRANSACTION_ROLLBACK",
"operations": [
// Initial state: Transaction manager with nested transactions and selective rollback
{
"type": "FILE_SETUP",
"files": {
"TransactionManager.ts": `
export class TransactionManager {
private state: Map<string, any> = new Map();
private snapshots: Map<string, any[]> = new Map();
private transactionDepth: number = 0;

async beginTransaction(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted before transaction');
this.transactionDepth++;
const snapshot = new Map(this.state);
const snapshotArray = this.snapshots.get(this.transactionDepth.toString()) || [];
snapshotArray.push(snapshot);
this.snapshots.set(this.transactionDepth.toString(), snapshotArray);
}

async commit(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted during commit');
if (this.transactionDepth > 0) {
this.snapshots.delete(this.transactionDepth.toString());
this.transactionDepth--;
}
}

async rollback(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
if (this.transactionDepth > 0) {
const snapshotArray = this.snapshots.get(this.transactionDepth.toString());
if (snapshotArray && snapshotArray.length > 0) {
const snapshot = snapshotArray.pop();
if (snapshot) this.state = new Map(snapshot);
}
this.transactionDepth--;
}
}

// Selective rollback based on keys
async selectiveRollback(keys: string[], signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
if (this.transactionDepth > 0) {
const snapshotArray = this.snapshots.get(this.transactionDepth.toString());
if (snapshotArray && snapshotArray.length > 0) {
const snapshot = snapshotArray.pop();
if (snapshot) {
keys.forEach(key => {
if (snapshot.has(key)) {
this.state.set(key, snapshot.get(key));
}
});
}
}
}
}

async update(key: string, value: any, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted during update');
await this.beginTransaction(signal);
try {
this.state.set(key, value);
await this.commit(signal);
} catch (error) {
await this.rollback(signal);
throw error;
}
}
}
`
}
```

Cline has a question:
Let's add a deferred execution scenario that tests how our system handles scheduled operations during transactions:

// Test deferred execution with transaction rollback
{
    id: "test-deferred-execution",
    type: "Scenario",
    description: "Deferred execution handling during transaction rollbacks",
    stages: [
        {
            stage: "DEFERRED_EXECUTION",
            operations: [
                // Initial state: Transaction manager with deferred operations
                {
                    type: "FILE_SETUP",
                    files: {
                        "DeferredTransactionManager.ts": `
                            export class DeferredTransactionManager {
                                private state: Map<string, any> = new Map();
                                private deferredOps: Map<number, Set<() => Promise<void>>> = new Map();
                                private transactionDepth: number = 0;

                                async beginTransaction(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
                                    if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted before transaction');
                                    this.transactionDepth++;
                                    this.deferredOps.set(this.transactionDepth, new Set());
                                }

                                async scheduleOperation(op: () => Promise<void>, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
                                    if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted during scheduling');
                                    const ops = this.deferredOps.get(this.transactionDepth);
                                    if (ops) ops.add(op);
                                }

                                async commit(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
                                    if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted during commit');
                                    const ops = this.deferredOps.get(this.transactionDepth);
                                    if (ops) {
                                        for (const op of ops) {
                                            if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted during deferred execution');
                                            await op();
                                        }
                                    }
                                    this.deferredOps.delete(this.transactionDepth);
                                    this.transactionDepth--;
                                }

                                async rollback(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
                                    // Cancel all deferred operations for this transaction
                                    this.deferredOps.delete(this.transactionDepth);
                                    this.transactionDepth--;
                                }

                                async update(key: string, value: any, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
                                    await this.beginTransaction(signal);
                                    try {
                                        // Schedule the update as a deferred operation
                                        await this.scheduleOperation(async () => {
                                            this.state.set(key, value);
                                        }, signal);
                                        await this.commit(signal);
                                    } catch (error) {
                                        await this.rollback(signal);
                                        throw error;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        `
                    }
                },
                // Simulate deferred operations with abort
                {
                    type: "DEFERRED_OPERATIONS",
                    operations: [
                        {
                            type: "TRANSACTION",
                            steps: [
                                {
                                    key: "key1",
                                    value: "value1",
                                    timing: "T0",
                                    deferredTiming: "T0+500ms"
                                },
                                {
                                    key: "key2",
                                    value: "value2",
                                    timing: "T0+100ms",
                                    deferredTiming: "T0+600ms"
                                },
                                {
                                    key: "key3",
                                    value: "value3",
                                    timing: "T0+200ms",
                                    abort: true
                                }
                            ]
                        }
                    ]
                }
            ],
            simulateFailure: {
                novaId: "Nova-Complete-1",
                failureType: "DEFERRED_OPERATION_ABORT",
                timing: "DURING_EXECUTION"
            }
        }
    ],
    expectedOutcome: {
        stateConsistency: "MAINTAINED",
        deferredCleanup: "COMPLETE",
        operationCancellation: "VERIFIED",
        maxLatency: "<700ms"
    }
}
This tests how our system handles deferred operations during transactions. Should we add more complex scheduling patterns or different execution timing scenarios?

### **Proposed Additional Deferred Execution Scenarios**

1. **Deferred Execution with Timeout Handling**:
- **Scenario**: Test how the system handles deferred operations with timeouts. For example, if a deferred operation takes too long or exceeds a defined threshold, it should be canceled or rescheduled. You can simulate this with a timeout mechanism that aborts the operation after a set period.

6. **Delayed Commit and Abort Handling**:

- **Goal**: Ensure that the system correctly handles delayed operations after a transaction has been committed and that the state is consistent if an abort occurs during the delay.

### **Expanded Version of the Current Scenario**

#### **Scenario: Deferred Execution with Timeout Handling and Prioritization**

```json
{
"id": "test-deferred-execution-priority-timeout",
"type": "Scenario",
"description": "Deferred execution handling with priority and timeout during transaction rollbacks",
"stages": [
{
"stage": "DEFERRED_EXECUTION_PRIORITY_TIMEOUT",
"operations": [
// Initial state: Deferred transaction manager with priority and timeout
{
"type": "FILE_SETUP",
"files": {
"DeferredTransactionManager.ts": `
export class DeferredTransactionManager {
private state: Map<string, any> = new Map();
private deferredOps: Map<number, Set<() => Promise<void>>> = new Map();
private transactionDepth: number = 0;

async beginTransaction(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted before transaction');
this.transactionDepth++;
this.deferredOps.set(this.transactionDepth, new Set());
}

async scheduleOperation(op: () => Promise<void>, signal?: AbortSignal, priority: number = 1): Promise<void> {
if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted during scheduling');
const ops = this.deferredOps.get(this.transactionDepth);
if (ops) {
ops.add({ op, priority });
}
}

async commit(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted during commit');
const ops = Array.from(this.deferredOps.get(this.transactionDepth) || []);
// Sort operations by priority
ops.sort((a, b) => b.priority - a.priority);

for (const { op } of ops) {
if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted during deferred execution');
await op();
}

this.deferredOps.delete(this.transactionDepth);
this.transactionDepth--;
}

async rollback(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
// Cancel all deferred operations for this transaction
this.deferredOps.delete(this.transactionDepth);
this.transactionDepth--;
}

async update(key: string, value: any, signal?: AbortSignal, priority: number = 1): Promise<void> {
await this.beginTransaction(signal);
try {
// Schedule the update as a deferred operation with a priority
await this.scheduleOperation(async () => {
this.state.set(key, value);
}, signal, priority);
await this.commit(signal);
} catch (error) {
await this.rollback(signal);
throw error;
}
}
}
`
}
},
// Simulate deferred operations with timeout handling and prioritization
{
"type": "DEFERRED_OPERATIONS",
"operations": [
{
"type": "TRANSACTION",
"steps": [
{
```

---

This additional complexity—prioritization of deferred operations and handling timeouts—will provide deeper insight into how your system can manage scheduled tasks, aborts, and transaction rollbacks.



Cline has a question:
Let's add a cross-transaction deferred execution scenario that tests how our system handles operations spanning multiple transactions:

// Test cross-transaction deferred operations
{
    id: "test-cross-transaction-deferred",
    type: "Scenario",
    description: "Deferred operations spanning multiple transactions",
    stages: [
        {
            stage: "CROSS_TRANSACTION_DEFERRED",
            operations: [
                // Initial state: Cross-transaction manager
                {
                    type: "FILE_SETUP",
                    files: {
                        "CrossTransactionManager.ts": `
                            export class CrossTransactionManager {
                                private state: Map<string, any> = new Map();
                                private deferredOps: Map<string, Set<{
                                    op: () => Promise<void>,
                                    transactionId: string,
                                    dependsOn?: string
                                }>> = new Map();
                                private activeTransactions: Set<string> = new Set();

                                async beginTransaction(transactionId: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
                                    if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted before transaction');
                                    this.activeTransactions.add(transactionId);
                                    this.deferredOps.set(transactionId, new Set());
                                }

                                async scheduleOperation(
                                    op: () => Promise<void>,
                                    transactionId: string,
                                    dependsOn?: string,
                                    signal?: AbortSignal
                                ): Promise<void> {
                                    if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted during scheduling');
                                    const ops = this.deferredOps.get(transactionId);
                                    if (ops) {
                                        ops.add({ op, transactionId, dependsOn });
                                    }
                                }

                                async commit(transactionId: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
                                    if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted during commit');
                                    const ops = this.deferredOps.get(transactionId);
                                    if (ops) {
                                        for (const { op, dependsOn } of ops) {
                                            if (dependsOn && this.activeTransactions.has(dependsOn)) {
                                                // Wait for dependent transaction
                                                continue;
                                            }
                                            if (signal?.aborted) throw new Error('Aborted during deferred execution');
                                            await op();
                                        }
                                    }
                                    this.deferredOps.delete(transactionId);
                                    this.activeTransactions.delete(transactionId);
                                }

                                async rollback(transactionId: string): Promise<void> {
                                    // Cancel operations for this transaction
                                    this.deferredOps.delete(transactionId);
                                    this.activeTransactions.delete(transactionId);
                                    
                                    // Cancel dependent operations in other transactions
                                    for (const [otherTxId, ops] of this.deferredOps.entries()) {
                                        const newOps = new Set(
                                            Array.from(ops).filter(op => op.dependsOn !== transactionId)
                                        );
                                        this.deferredOps.set(otherTxId, newOps);
                                    }
                                }

                                async update(
                                    key: string,
                                    value: any,
                                    transactionId: string,
                                    dependsOn?: string,
                                    signal?: AbortSignal
                                ): Promise<void> {
                                    await this.beginTransaction(transactionId, signal);
                                    try {
                                        await this.scheduleOperation(
                                            async () => { this.state.set(key, value); },
                                            transactionId,
                                            dependsOn,
                                            signal
                                        );
                                        await this.commit(transactionId, signal);
                                    } catch (error) {
                                        await this.rollback(transactionId);
                                        throw error;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        `
                    }
                },
                // Simulate cross-transaction operations
                {
                    type: "CROSS_TRANSACTION_OPERATIONS",
                    operations: [
                        {
                            type: "TRANSACTION",
                            id: "tx1",
                            steps: [
                                {
                                    key: "key1",
                                    value: "value1",
                                    timing: "T0"
                                }
                            ]
                        },
                        {
                            type: "TRANSACTION",
                            id: "tx2",
                            dependsOn: "tx1",
                            steps: [
                                {
                                    key: "key2",
                                    value: "value2",
                                    timing: "T0+100ms",
                                    abort: true
                                }
                            ]
                        }
                    ]
                }
            ],
            simulateFailure: {
                novaId: "Nova-Complete-1",
                failureType: "CROSS_TRANSACTION_ABORT",
                timing: "DURING_DEPENDENT_EXECUTION"
            }
        }
    ],
    expectedOutcome: {
        stateConsistency: "MAINTAINED",
        dependencyHandling: "CORRECT",
        crossTransactionCleanup: "COMPLETE",
        maxLatency: "<800ms"
    }
}
This tests how our system handles deferred operations that span multiple transactions with dependencies. Should we add more complex dependency patterns or different cross-transaction scenarios?
